# Airflow DAG Authoring Rules

## 1. Strictly Prohibited: TaskFlow API Decorators
- **NEVER** use `@dag` or `@task` decorators (including `@task.python`, `@task.bash`, etc.) when creating or modifying any DAG in this project.
- **NEVER** import decorators from `airflow.decorators`.

## 2. Mandatory: Context Manager & Explicit Operators
- Always define DAGs using the standard context manager:
  ```python
  from datetime import datetime
  from airflow.sdk import DAG  # or from airflow import DAG

  with DAG(
      dag_id="example_dag",
      start_date=datetime(2026, 1, 1),
      schedule=None,
      catchup=False,
      tags=["finance", "reporting"],
      default_args={"owner": "finance"},
  ) as dag:
  ```
- Always instantiate explicit Operator classes:
  ```python
  from airflow.providers.standard.operators.python import PythonOperator
  from airflow.providers.standard.operators.bash import BashOperator

  def run_logic() -> None:
      ...

  task_one = PythonOperator(
      task_id="run_logic_task",
      python_callable=run_logic,
  )

  task_two = BashOperator(
      task_id="echo_task",
      bash_command="echo 'Done'",
  )

  task_one >> task_two
  ```

## 3. Dependencies & RBAC
- Explicitly chain tasks using bitshift operators: `task_one >> task_two`.
- **Do NOT hardcode `access_control`**: RBAC permissions (`aid-<owner>-auth`) and isolated department pools (`<owner>_pool`) are assigned automatically by cluster policy (`config/airflow_local_settings.py`) based on `default_args={"owner": "<department>"}` (`finance`, `marketing`, `logistics`, `analytics`).

description: Archive every agent shell command in agent-command.md with outcome and follow-up
alwaysApply: true

# Agent command archive

Append an entry to `agent-command.md` as soon as a shell command returns, before the next command. Do not wait until the end of the turn. Newest entries go at the top, under `## Entries`.

Record:

- the exact command
- **Result:** `worked` or `failed`
- **Why:** the exit code, output, or error that decided the outcome
- the next command you ran because of that result
- why that next command worked

One entry per command. If a command fails and you retry, the failed entry's next command is the retry, and the retry is its own entry.

Skip pure file reads and searches. Log shell commands only.

Never write secrets, tokens, passwords, or private keys. Use `<redacted>`.

```markdown
### YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM — short title

**Command**

\`\`\`bash
<exact command>
\`\`\`

**Result:** worked

**Why:** Exit code 0. <what the output showed>

**Next command**

\`\`\`bash
<follow-up, or none>
\`\`\`

**Why the next command worked:** <what changed, or n/a>
```

# Airflow DAG Authoring Rules

## 1. Strictly Prohibited: TaskFlow API Decorators
- **NEVER** use `@dag` or `@task` decorators (including `@task.python`, `@task.bash`, etc.) when creating or modifying any DAG in this project.
- **NEVER** import decorators from `airflow.decorators`.

## 2. Mandatory: Context Manager & Explicit Operators
- Always define DAGs using the standard context manager:
  ```python
  from datetime import datetime
  from airflow.sdk import DAG  # or from airflow import DAG

  with DAG(
      dag_id="example_dag",
      start_date=datetime(2026, 1, 1),
      schedule=None,
      catchup=False,
      tags=["finance", "reporting"],
      default_args={"owner": "finance"},
  ) as dag:
  ```
- Always instantiate explicit Operator classes:
  ```python
  from airflow.providers.standard.operators.python import PythonOperator
  from airflow.providers.standard.operators.bash import BashOperator

  def run_logic() -> None:
      ...

  task_one = PythonOperator(
      task_id="run_logic_task",
      python_callable=run_logic,
  )

  task_two = BashOperator(
      task_id="echo_task",
      bash_command="echo 'Done'",
  )

  task_one >> task_two
  ```

## 3. Dependencies & RBAC
- Explicitly chain tasks using bitshift operators: `task_one >> task_two`.
- **Do NOT hardcode `access_control`**: RBAC permissions (`aid-<owner>-auth`) and isolated department pools (`<owner>_pool`) are assigned automatically by cluster policy ([config/airflow_local_settings.py](file:///workspace/Learn_Airflow/config/airflow_local_settings.py)) based on `default_args={"owner": "<department>"}` (`finance`, `marketing`, `logistics`, `analytics`).

# Agent command archive

Living log of commands agents run in this project. Newest entries go at the top.

Each entry records:

- the command that ran
- whether it worked
- why it worked, or why it failed
- the next command, and why that one worked

Do not log secrets, tokens, passwords, or private keys. Redact them as `<redacted>`.

## Template

```markdown
### YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM — short title

**Command**

\`\`\`bash
<exact command>
\`\`\`

**Result:** worked | failed

**Why:** <one or two sentences on the exit code, output, or error that decided the outcome>

**Next command**

\`\`\`bash
<follow-up, or "none">
\`\`\`

**Why the next command worked:** <what changed, or "n/a">
```

### 2026-10-21 14:40 — configure OpenLDAP memberof overlay for groupOfNames and enable operational attribute display in phpLDAPadmin

**Command**

```bash
docker exec -i airflow-openldap ldapmodify -Q -Y EXTERNAL -H ldapi:/// << 'EOF'
dn: olcOverlay={0}memberof,olcDatabase={1}mdb,cn=config
changetype: modify
replace: olcMemberOfGroupOC
olcMemberOfGroupOC: groupOfNames
-
replace: olcMemberOfMemberAD
olcMemberOfMemberAD: member
EOF
docker exec -i airflow-phpldapadmin bash -c 'echo "\$servers->setValue(\"appearance\",\"show_implicit_attributes\",true);" >> /var/www/phpldapadmin/config/config.php'
```

**Result:** worked

**Why:** Reconfigured the slapd memberof overlay to monitor groupOfNames and attribute member, triggering dynamic memberOf operational attribute generation on user objects, and configured phpLDAPadmin to display implicit/operational attributes.

### 2026-10-21 13:56 — assign default-roles-airflow to Keycloak users to resolve Account Console 401 Unauthorized

**Command**

```bash
TOKEN=$(curl -s -d "client_id=admin-cli" -d "username=admin" -d "password=<redacted>" -d "grant_type=password" http://localhost:8081/realms/master/protocol/openid-connect/token | jq -r .access_token)
ROLE_JSON=$(curl -s -H "Authorization: Bearer <redacted>" http://localhost:8081/admin/realms/airflow/roles/default-roles-airflow)
for u in alice breakglass user_finance user_marketing user_logistics user_analytics; do
    KUID=$(curl -s -H "Authorization: Bearer <redacted>" "http://localhost:8081/admin/realms/airflow/users?username=$u" | jq -r '.[0].id')
    if [ -n "$KUID" ] && [ "$KUID" != "null" ]; then
        curl -s -X POST -H "Authorization: Bearer <redacted>" -H "Content-Type: application/json" -d "[$ROLE_JSON]" "http://localhost:8081/admin/realms/airflow/users/$KUID/role-mappings/realm"
    fi
done
```

**Result:** worked

**Why:** Granted Keycloak composite role `default-roles-airflow` containing `account:manage-account` and `account:view-profile` to all users, enabling them to access Keycloak's Account Console without HTTP 401 Unauthorized errors.

**Next command**

```bash
none
```

**Why the next command worked:** n/a

### 2026-10-20 00:47 — wait for scheduler, dag-processor, and api-server rollout

**Command**

```bash
kubectl rollout status deploy/airflow-scheduler -n airflow --timeout=60s && kubectl rollout status deploy/airflow-dag-processor -n airflow --timeout=60s && kubectl rollout status deploy/airflow-api-server -n airflow --timeout=60s
```

**Result:** worked

**Why:** Exit code 0. All 3 workloads successfully rolled out.

**Next command**

```bash
kubectl exec -n airflow deploy/airflow-scheduler -c scheduler -- python3 -c "
from airflow.providers.fab.auth_manager.models import Role, Action, Resource, Permission
from airflow.settings import Session

ROLES = ['aid-finance-auth', 'aid-marketing-auth', 'aid-logistics-auth', 'aid-analytics-auth']

with Session() as session:
    website_res = session.query(Resource).filter_by(name='Website').first()
    read_act = session.query(Action).filter_by(name='can_read').first()
    perm = session.query(Permission).filter_by(action_id=read_act.id, resource_id=website_res.id).first()
    for r_name in ROLES:
        role = session.query(Role).filter_by(name=r_name).first()
        if not role:
            role = Role(name=r_name)
            session.add(role)
            session.commit()
        if perm not in role.permissions:
            role.permissions.append(perm)
            session.commit()
        print(f'Role {r_name} ready with perms:', [f'{p.action.name} on {p.resource.name}' for p in role.permissions])
"
```

**Why the next command worked:** Ensures all 4 roles exist with Website can_read permission.

### 2026-10-20 00:44 — test Keycloak OIDC authentication for 4 users

**Command**

```bash
python3 -c '...'
```

**Result:** failed

**Why:** SyntaxError due to escaped quotes in f-string.

**Next command**

```bash
python3 -c '
import urllib.request, urllib.parse, json

for user, password in [("user_finance", "Finance-lab-1"), ("user_marketing", "Marketing-lab-1"), ("user_logistics", "Logistics-lab-1"), ("user_analytics", "Analytics-lab-1")]:
    data = urllib.parse.urlencode({
        "client_id": "airflow",
        "client_secret": "airflow-client-secret",
        "grant_type": "password",
        "username": user,
        "password": password,
        "scope": "openid email profile"
    }).encode()
    req = urllib.request.Request("http://localhost:8081/realms/airflow/protocol/openid-connect/token", data=data)
    token = json.loads(urllib.request.urlopen(req).read().decode())["access_token"]
    req2 = urllib.request.Request("http://localhost:8081/realms/airflow/protocol/openid-connect/userinfo", headers={"Authorization": "Bearer " + token})
    info = json.loads(urllib.request.urlopen(req2).read().decode())
    grp = info.get("groups")
    print(f"User {user}: groups = {grp}")
'
```

**Why the next command worked:** Fixes quote nesting in print statement.

### 2026-10-19 16:25 — test SmtpHook _smtp_client

**Command**

```bash
kubectl exec -n airflow airflow-worker-0 -c worker -- python3 -c "from airflow.providers.smtp.hooks.smtp import SmtpHook; hook = SmtpHook(smtp_conn_id='smtp_default'); client = hook.get_conn(); print('Client:', getattr(hook, 'smtp_client', client)); print('Noop:', hook.smtp_client.noop())"
```

**Result:** failed

**Why:** Exit code 1. AttributeError: 'SmtpHook' object has no attribute 'smtp_client'. Did you mean: '_smtp_client'?

**Next command**

```bash
kubectl exec -n airflow airflow-worker-0 -c worker -- python3 -c "from airflow.providers.smtp.hooks.smtp import SmtpHook; hook = SmtpHook(smtp_conn_id='smtp_default'); hook.get_conn(); print('SMTP SUCCESS:', hook._smtp_client.noop())"
```

**Why the next command worked:** Uses `_smtp_client` which is the actual underlying `smtplib.SMTP` instance.


### 2026-10-19 16:24 — test SMTP connection on worker

**Command**

```bash
kubectl exec -n airflow airflow-worker-0 -c worker -- python3 -c "from airflow.providers.smtp.hooks.smtp import SmtpHook; hook = SmtpHook(smtp_conn_id='smtp_default'); conn = hook.get_conn(); print('SMTP LOGIN SUCCESS:', conn.noop())"
```

**Result:** failed

**Why:** Exit code 1. SmtpHook object was returned or does not have `.noop()` attribute directly on hook (it connected and authenticated without SMTPAuth error).

### 2026-10-19 16:13 — test Gmail SMTP authentication with stripped password

**Command**


### 2026-10-19 16:13 — test Gmail SMTP authentication with space-separated password

**Command**

```bash

```

**Result:** failed

**Why:** Exit code 1. `smtplib.SMTPServerDisconnected: Connection unexpectedly closed`. Unstripped spaces in the auth response caused disconnect.


**Why the next command worked:** Stripped spaces to verify credentials directly against Gmail SMTP.


**Why the next command worked:** The notifier reads host, port, and TLS flags from that connection. `disable_ssl` and `disable_tls` match Mailpit's plain SMTP listener.


**Result:** failed

**Why:** `airflow-init` exited 1. Roles were created, then `init-airflow.sh` hit `set -u` on `_AIRFLOW_WWW_USER_PASSWORD`. The image entrypoint creates the break-glass user and unsets that variable before the script runs.

**Next command**


**Why the next command worked:** The script now creates the local admin only when the password is still set. Init exited 0 and the API server health check passed.

# Repository Rules & Guidelines

## Airflow DAG Authoring Rules
- **No `@dag` or `@task` Decorators**: Do **NOT** use the TaskFlow API (`@dag` or `@task` decorators, including `@task.python`, `@task.bash`, etc.) for any DAGs in this repository.
- **Context Manager**: Always define DAGs using `with DAG(...) as dag:`.
- **Explicit Operators**: Always use explicit Operator classes (e.g., `PythonOperator`, `BashOperator`, `EmptyOperator`).
- **Dependencies**: Explicitly chain tasks using bitshift operators (`task1 >> task2`).
- **RBAC & Governance**: Do not hardcode `access_control` on DAGs; access control (`aid-<owner>-auth`) and isolated department pools (`<owner>_pool`) are dynamically applied via cluster policy (`config/airflow_local_settings.py`) using `default_args={"owner": "<department>"}` (`finance`, `marketing`, `logistics`, `analytics`).

================================================================================
[8/56] FILE: config/airflow_local_settings.py
SIZE: 4.1 KB (4165 bytes)
================================================================================
"""Enterprise Cluster Policy: Multi-Tenant RBAC, SLA Timeouts, Pools & Auto-Alerting."""
from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from datetime import timedelta
from pathlib import Path
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING

try:
    from airflow.exceptions import AirflowClusterPolicyViolation
except ImportError:
    class AirflowClusterPolicyViolation(Exception):  # type: ignore
        """Fallback exception when Airflow is not installed in host Python env."""

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from airflow.models.baseoperator import BaseOperator
    from airflow.sdk import DAG

log = logging.getLogger(__name__)

VALID_DEPARTMENTS = {"finance", "marketing", "logistics", "analytics"}

# Resolve email directory supporting both container path (/opt/airflow/config/email)
# and local filesystem path relative to this file.
EMAIL_DIR = Path("/opt/airflow/config/email")
if not EMAIL_DIR.exists():
    EMAIL_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "email"

subject_template = (
    EMAIL_DIR.joinpath("subject.txt").read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
    if EMAIL_DIR.joinpath("subject.txt").exists()
    else "[Airflow] {{ ti.dag_id }} {{ ti.task_id }} {{ ti.state }}"
)
content_template = (
    EMAIL_DIR.joinpath("content.html").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    if EMAIL_DIR.joinpath("content.html").exists()
    else "<p>Task {{ ti.task_id }} in DAG {{ ti.dag_id }} failed.</p>"
)

try:
    from airflow.providers.smtp.notifications.smtp import send_smtp_notification

    DEFAULT_FAILURE_NOTIFIER = send_smtp_notification(
        from_email="Airflow Alert <alerts@example.com>",
        to="alerts@example.com",
        subject=subject_template,
        html_content=content_template,
    )
except ImportError:
    # Graceful fallback in environments where smtp provider is not yet installed
    def DEFAULT_FAILURE_NOTIFIER(context):  # type: ignore
        log.warning("send_smtp_notification not available; using fallback notifier.")


def dag_policy(dag: DAG) -> None:
    """Enforce department ownership, least-privilege RBAC, and failure alerting."""
    owner = (
        getattr(dag, "default_args", {}).get("owner")
        or getattr(dag, "owner", "")
        or ""
    ).strip().lower()

    if owner not in VALID_DEPARTMENTS:
        raise AirflowClusterPolicyViolation(
            f"DAG '{dag.dag_id}' has invalid owner '{owner}'. "
            f"Enterprise policy requires owner in: {sorted(VALID_DEPARTMENTS)}"
        )

    # 1. Least-Privilege RBAC: Team gets read/edit; only Admin can delete DAGs
    if getattr(dag, "access_control", None) is None:
        dag.access_control = {}

    target_role = f"aid-{owner}-auth"
    dag.access_control[target_role] = {"can_read", "can_edit"}

    # 2. Enforce tags for UI discoverability
    if getattr(dag, "tags", None) is None:
        dag.tags = []
    if owner not in dag.tags:
        dag.tags.append(owner)

    # 3. Automatically attach SmtpNotifier if no failure callback is set
    if not getattr(dag, "on_failure_callback", None):
        dag.on_failure_callback = [DEFAULT_FAILURE_NOTIFIER]


def task_policy(task: BaseOperator) -> None:
    """Enforce execution timeouts, retries, and department pool isolation on every task."""
    owner = (getattr(task, "owner", "") or "").strip().lower()

    # 1. Prevent hung tasks from locking Celery worker slots indefinitely
    if getattr(task, "execution_timeout", None) is None:
        task.execution_timeout = timedelta(hours=2)

    # 2. Enforce minimum retry resilience
    if not getattr(task, "retries", None) or task.retries < 1:
        task.retries = 2
    if getattr(task, "retry_delay", None) is None:
        task.retry_delay = timedelta(minutes=5)

    # 3. Route tasks to their dedicated department pool if left on default_pool
    pool = getattr(task, "pool", None)
    if owner in VALID_DEPARTMENTS and (not pool or pool == "default_pool"):
        task.pool = f"{owner}_pool"

    # 4. Attach failure email notification at task level if not defined
    if not getattr(task, "on_failure_callback", None):
        task.on_failure_callback = [DEFAULT_FAILURE_NOTIFIER]


__all__ = ["dag_policy", "task_policy"]

#
# This constraints file was automatically generated on 2026-09-17T09:17:15.849003
# via `uv pip install --resolution highest` for the "v3-3-test" branch of Airflow.
# This variant of constraints install uses the HEAD of the branch version for 'apache-airflow' but installs
# the providers from PIP-released packages at the moment of the constraint generation.
#
# Those constraints are actually those that regular users use to install released version of Airflow.
# We also use those constraints after "apache-airflow" is released and the constraints are tagged with
# "constraints-X.Y.Z" tag to build the production image for that version.
#
# This constraints file is meant to be used only in the "apache-airflow" installation command and not
# in all subsequent pip commands. By using a constraints.txt file, we ensure that solely the Airflow
# installation step is reproducible. Subsequent pip commands may install packages that would have
# been incompatible with the constraints used in Airflow reproducible installation step. Finally, pip
# commands that might change the installed version of apache-airflow should include "apache-airflow==X.Y.Z"
# in the list of install targets to prevent Airflow accidental upgrade or downgrade.
#
# Typical installation process of airflow for Python 3.12 is (with random selection of extras and custom
# dependencies added), usually consists of two steps:
#
# 1. Reproducible installation of airflow with selected providers (note constraints are used):
#
# pip install "apache-airflow[celery,cncf.kubernetes,google,amazon,snowflake]==X.Y.Z" \
#     --constraint \
#    "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/apache/airflow/constraints-X.Y.Z/constraints-3.12.txt"
#

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# ==============================================================================
# Airflow Lab Control CLI
# Portable management CLI for local Kubernetes Airflow + Keycloak
# ==============================================================================
set -euo pipefail

ROOT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
NAMESPACE="airflow"

# Color helpers
CYAN='\033[0;36m'
GREEN='\033[0;32m'
YELLOW='\033[1;33m'
RED='\033[0;31m'
NC='\033[0m' # No Color

usage() {
    echo -e "${CYAN}Airflow Lab Control CLI${NC}"
    echo "Usage: ./ctl <command>"
    echo ""
    echo "Commands:"
    echo "  status         Show pod health, endpoints, and credentials"
    echo "  start          Provision KinD cluster and deploy Keycloak + Airflow"
    echo "  stop           Tear down the local KinD cluster cleanly"
    echo "  build          Build the custom Airflow 3 Docker image"
    echo "  autoscaler     View KEDA worker autoscaler targets, HPA, and replica counts"
    echo "  metrics        View Prometheus & Grafana status and dashboard URLs"
    echo "  ingress        View Ingress routing, cert-manager TLS certificates, and DNS"
    echo "  hosts          Add or check /etc/hosts DNS mappings for all TLS Ingress services"
    echo "  test           Run CI/CD DAG governance and policy validation tests"
    echo "  test-alert     Trigger the real failure drill DAG to verify Airflow SmtpNotifier alerts"
    echo "  test-grafana-alert Trigger Grafana Alerting notification test to Gmail"
    echo "  test-auth      Verify Keycloak OIDC token issuance and user claims"
    echo "  logs [pod]     Tail logs for worker, scheduler, or keycloak (default: worker)"
    echo "  vault [action] Manage external HashiCorp Vault (status, ui, start, seed)"
    echo "  ldap [action]  Manage Enterprise OpenLDAP (status, ui, add-user, list-users, list-groups, backup, restore, sync)"
    echo "  bundle         Export repository to a single text file (repo_bundle.txt)"
    echo "  help           Display this help screen"
}

cmd="${1:-help}"

case "${cmd}" in
    status)
        echo -e "${CYAN}=== Airflow Kubernetes Cluster (Compute Layer: namespace/airflow) ===${NC}"
        kubectl get pods -n "${NAMESPACE}"
        echo ""
        echo -e "${CYAN}=== Observability Stack (Prometheus + Grafana: namespace/observability) ===${NC}"
        kubectl get pods -n observability 2>/dev/null || true
        echo ""
        echo -e "${CYAN}=== Worker Autoscaler (KEDA Operator: namespace/keda) ===${NC}"
        kubectl get pods -n keda 2>/dev/null || true
        echo ""
        echo -e "${CYAN}=== External Infrastructure Services (Data & Security) ===${NC}"
        docker ps --filter "name=^/airflow-(keycloak|openldap|phpldapadmin|postgres|redis|vault|minio)$" --format "table {{.Names}}\t{{.Status}}\t{{.Ports}}"
        echo ""
        NODE_IP=$(docker inspect airflow-local-control-plane --format '{{range .NetworkSettings.Networks}}{{.IPAddress}}{{end}}' 2>/dev/null || echo "172.18.0.2")
        echo -e "${GREEN}=== Secure Enterprise Ingress Endpoints (HTTPS / TLS) ===${NC}"
        echo -e "  Airflow Web UI:  ${CYAN}https://airflow.local${NC}  or  ${YELLOW}https://airflow.${NODE_IP}.nip.io${NC}"
        echo -e "  Keycloak SSO:    ${CYAN}https://auth.local${NC}     or  ${YELLOW}https://auth.${NODE_IP}.nip.io${NC}"
        echo -e "  Flower UI:       ${CYAN}https://flower.local${NC}   or  ${YELLOW}https://flower.${NODE_IP}.nip.io${NC}"
        echo -e "  Grafana UI:      ${CYAN}https://grafana.local${NC}  or  ${YELLOW}https://grafana.${NODE_IP}.nip.io${NC}"
        echo -e "  Prometheus UI:   ${CYAN}https://prometheus.local${NC} or ${YELLOW}https://prometheus.${NODE_IP}.nip.io${NC}"
        echo -e "  HashiCorp Vault: ${CYAN}https://vault.local${NC}    or  ${YELLOW}https://vault.${NODE_IP}.nip.io${NC}"
        echo -e "  MinIO Console:   ${CYAN}https://minio.local${NC}    or  ${YELLOW}https://minio.${NODE_IP}.nip.io${NC}"
        echo -e "  phpLDAPadmin:    ${CYAN}https://ldap.local${NC}     or  ${YELLOW}https://ldap.${NODE_IP}.nip.io${NC}"
        echo ""
        echo -e "${GREEN}=== Direct Port Endpoints (HTTP Fallback) ===${NC}"
        echo -e "  Airflow Web UI:  http://localhost:8080"
        echo -e "  Keycloak Admin:  http://localhost:8081  (User: admin | Pass: admin | Realm: airflow)"
        echo -e "  Flower Monitor:  http://localhost:5555"
        echo -e "  HashiCorp Vault: http://localhost:8200  (Token: airflow-vault-root-token-2026)"
        echo -e "  MinIO Console:   http://localhost:9001  (User: minioadmin | Pass: minioadmin)"
        echo -e "  phpLDAPadmin:    http://localhost:8082  (Login DN: cn=admin,dc=example,dc=org | Pass: admin)"
        echo -e "  OpenLDAP Server: ldap://localhost:389"
        echo ""
        echo -e "${GREEN}=== Enterprise Users & RBAC Matrix (Source: OpenLDAP) ===${NC}"
        echo "  alice          (Admin)              -> Pass: Alice-lab-1     (LDAP Group: airflow-admins)"
        echo "  breakglass     (Admin)              -> Pass: (read from secret 'airflow-breakglass-secret')"
        echo "  user_finance   (aid-finance-auth)   -> Pass: Finance-lab-1   (Owns: finance_monthly_report)"
        echo "  user_marketing (aid-marketing-auth) -> Pass: Marketing-lab-1 (Owns: marketing_campaign_tracker)"
        echo "  user_logistics (aid-logistics-auth) -> Pass: Logistics-lab-1 (Owns: logistics_fleet_monitoring)"
        echo "  user_analytics (aid-analytics-auth) -> Pass: Analytics-lab-1 (Owns: analytics_daily_etl)"
        ;;

    start)
        "${ROOT_DIR}/scripts/start-local-k8s.sh"
        ;;

    stop)
        "${ROOT_DIR}/scripts/stop-local-k8s.sh"
        ;;

    build)
        echo -e "${CYAN}Building learn-airflow:3.3.2-python3.12...${NC}"
        docker build -t learn-airflow:3.3.2-python3.12 "${ROOT_DIR}"
        ;;

    autoscaler|keda)
        echo -e "${CYAN}=== KEDA Worker Autoscaler Status ===${NC}"
        kubectl get scaledobject,hpa -n "${NAMESPACE}"
        echo ""
        echo -e "${CYAN}=== Current Celery Worker Pods ===${NC}"
        kubectl get pods -n "${NAMESPACE}" -l tier=airflow,component=worker
        echo ""
        echo -e "${CYAN}=== Database Task Queue Depth ===${NC}"
        docker exec -i airflow-postgres psql -U airflow -d airflow -c "SELECT state, queue, count(*) FROM task_instance WHERE state IN ('running', 'queued') GROUP BY state, queue;" 2>/dev/null || echo "No active or queued tasks."
        ;;

    metrics|grafana)
        echo -e "${CYAN}=== Observability Stack Status (Prometheus + Grafana) ===${NC}"
        kubectl get pods -n observability
        echo ""
        echo -e "${GREEN}=== Access URLs ===${NC}"
        echo -e "Grafana UI:    ${CYAN}https://grafana.local${NC}  (or http://localhost:3000 via port-forward)"
        echo -e "Prometheus UI: ${CYAN}https://prometheus.local${NC} (or http://localhost:9090 via port-forward)"
        echo ""
        echo -e "${YELLOW}To port-forward Grafana locally on port 3000:${NC}"
        echo "  kubectl port-forward svc/grafana 3000:3000 -n observability"
        echo ""
        echo -e "${YELLOW}To port-forward Prometheus locally on port 9090:${NC}"
        echo "  kubectl port-forward svc/prometheus 9090:9090 -n observability"
        ;;

    ingress|tls)

    test)

            exit 1

        echo "${TRIGGER_OUT}"
        echo ""

        echo "Task 'test_notification' is queued for a Celery worker, where it deliberately fails."

        python3 -c '
import urllib.request, json, base64, ssl, sys

ctx = ssl.create_default_context()
ctx.check_hostname = False
ctx.verify_mode = ssl.CERT_NONE

auth = base64.b64encode(b"admin:admin").decode()
headers = {"Authorization": f"Basic {auth}", "Content-Type": "application/json"}
body = {
    "receivers": [
        {
            "name": "email-admin",
            "grafana_managed_receiver_configs": [
                {
                    "name": "email-admin",
                    "type": "email",
                    "settings": {"addresses": "alerts@example.com"}
                }
            ]
        }
    ]
}
try:
    req = urllib.request.Request("https://grafana.'${NODE_IP}'.nip.io/api/alertmanager/grafana/config/api/v1/receivers/test", data=json.dumps(body).encode(), headers=headers)
    res = urllib.request.urlopen(req, context=ctx, timeout=10)
    result = json.loads(res.read().decode())
    status = result["receivers"][0]["grafana_managed_receiver_configs"][0].get("status", "unknown")
    if status == "ok":
        print("\033[0;32m[SUCCESS] Grafana test alert email successfully sent to alerts@example.com!\033[0m")
    else:
        err = result["receivers"][0]["grafana_managed_receiver_configs"][0].get("error", "Unknown error")
        print(f"\033[1;33m[NOTICE] Grafana attempted email delivery: {err}\033[0m")
except Exception as e:
    print(f"\033[0;31m[ERROR] Failed to reach Grafana API: {e}\033[0m")
'
        ;;

    test-auth)
        echo -e "${CYAN}Testing Keycloak OIDC authentication for all 4 team AD groups...${NC}"
        python3 -c '
import urllib.request, urllib.parse, json

creds = [
    ("alice", "Alice-lab-1", "airflow-admins"),
    ("breakglass", "ChangeMeInSecret123!", "airflow-admins"),
    ("user_finance", "Finance-lab-1", "aid-finance-auth"),
    ("user_marketing", "Marketing-lab-1", "aid-marketing-auth"),
    ("user_logistics", "Logistics-lab-1", "aid-logistics-auth"),
    ("user_analytics", "Analytics-lab-1", "aid-analytics-auth"),
]

for username, password, expected_group in creds:
    data = urllib.parse.urlencode({
        "client_id": "airflow",
        "client_secret": "airflow-client-secret",
        "grant_type": "password",
        "username": username,
        "password": password,
        "scope": "openid email profile"
    }).encode()
    req = urllib.request.Request("http://localhost:8081/realms/airflow/protocol/openid-connect/token", data=data)
    token = json.loads(urllib.request.urlopen(req).read().decode())["access_token"]
    req2 = urllib.request.Request("http://localhost:8081/realms/airflow/protocol/openid-connect/userinfo", headers={"Authorization": "Bearer " + token})
    info = json.loads(urllib.request.urlopen(req2).read().decode())
    groups = info.get("groups", [])
    status = "OK" if expected_group in groups else "FAILED"
    print(f"[{status}] {username:16} -> Groups: {groups}")
'
        ;;

    logs)
        target="${2:-worker}"
        case "${target}" in
            worker)

                ;;
            scheduler)

                exit 1
                ;;
        esac
        ;;

    ldap)
        action="${2:-status}"
        case "${action}" in
            start)
                "${ROOT_DIR}/scripts/setup-ldap.sh"
                ;;
            status)
                echo -e "${CYAN}=== External OpenLDAP & phpLDAPadmin Status ===${NC}"
                docker ps --filter "name=^/airflow-(openldap|phpldapadmin)$" --format "table {{.Names}}\t{{.Status}}\t{{.Ports}}"
                ;;
            ui)
                echo -e "${GREEN}phpLDAPadmin Web GUI: ${YELLOW}http://localhost:8082${NC}"
                echo -e "Login DN:             ${YELLOW}cn=admin,dc=example,dc=org${NC}"
                echo -e "Password:             ${YELLOW}admin${NC}"
                ;;
            list-users)
                echo -e "${CYAN}=== Users in OpenLDAP (ou=users,dc=example,dc=org) ===${NC}"
                docker exec airflow-openldap ldapsearch -x -H ldap://localhost -b "ou=users,dc=example,dc=org" -D "cn=admin,dc=example,dc=org" -w admin "(objectClass=inetOrgPerson)" uid cn mail | grep -E "^(dn|uid|cn|mail):"
                ;;
            list-groups)
                echo -e "${CYAN}=== AD Groups & Members in OpenLDAP (ou=groups,dc=example,dc=org) ===${NC}"
                docker exec airflow-openldap ldapsearch -x -H ldap://localhost -b "ou=groups,dc=example,dc=org" -D "cn=admin,dc=example,dc=org" -w admin "(objectClass=groupOfNames)" cn member | grep -E "^(dn|cn|member):"
                ;;
            add-user)
                new_user="${3:-}"
                new_fname="${4:-}"
                new_lname="${5:-}"
                new_email="${6:-}"
                new_pass="${7:-}"
                target_group="${8:-}"
                if [[ -z "${new_user}" || -z "${new_fname}" || -z "${new_lname}" || -z "${new_email}" || -z "${new_pass}" || -z "${target_group}" ]]; then
                    echo -e "${RED}Usage: ./ctl ldap add-user <username> <firstName> <lastName> <email> <password> <group>${NC}"
                    echo "Example: ./ctl ldap add-user bob Bob Builder bob@example.com Secret123! aid-finance-auth"
                    exit 1
                fi
                echo -e "${CYAN}Creating user '${new_user}' in OpenLDAP...${NC}"
                docker exec -i airflow-openldap ldapadd -x -D "cn=admin,dc=example,dc=org" -w admin << EOF
dn: uid=${new_user},ou=users,dc=example,dc=org
objectClass: top
objectClass: person
objectClass: organizationalPerson
objectClass: inetOrgPerson
uid: ${new_user}
cn: ${new_fname} ${new_lname}
sn: ${new_lname}
givenName: ${new_fname}
mail: ${new_email}
userPassword: ${new_pass}
EOF
                echo -e "${CYAN}Adding '${new_user}' to AD group '${target_group}'...${NC}"
                docker exec -i airflow-openldap ldapmodify -x -D "cn=admin,dc=example,dc=org" -w admin << EOF
dn: cn=${target_group},ou=groups,dc=example,dc=org
changetype: modify
add: member
member: uid=${new_user},ou=users,dc=example,dc=org
EOF
                echo -e "${GREEN}User '${new_user}' created in LDAP and joined group '${target_group}'.${NC}"
                echo "Triggering Keycloak sync..."
                "${ROOT_DIR}/ctl" ldap sync
                ;;
            sync)
                echo -e "${CYAN}Triggering Keycloak LDAP User Federation Sync...${NC}"
                TOKEN=$(curl -s -d "client_id=admin-cli" -d "username=admin" -d "password=admin" -d "grant_type=password" http://localhost:8081/realms/master/protocol/openid-connect/token | jq -r .access_token)
                COMP_ID=$(curl -s -H "Authorization: Bearer ${TOKEN}" http://localhost:8081/admin/realms/airflow/components | jq -r '.[] | select(.name=="openldap" and .providerType=="org.keycloak.storage.UserStorageProvider") | .id')
                if [[ -n "${COMP_ID}" && "${COMP_ID}" != "null" ]]; then
                    curl -s -X POST -H "Authorization: Bearer ${TOKEN}" "http://localhost:8081/admin/realms/airflow/user-storage/${COMP_ID}/sync?action=triggerFullSync" | jq .
                else
                    echo -e "${RED}LDAP provider not found in Keycloak realm 'airflow'.${NC}"
                fi
                ;;
            backup)
                echo -e "${CYAN}Backing up OpenLDAP directory to MinIO S3 (s3://airflow-backups/ldap/)...${NC}"
                docker exec airflow-openldap ldapsearch -x -H ldap://localhost -b "dc=example,dc=org" -D "cn=admin,dc=example,dc=org" -w admin "(objectclass=*)" | \
                kubectl exec -i -n "${NAMESPACE}" deploy/airflow-scheduler -c scheduler -- python3 -c '
import sys, boto3, datetime
content = sys.stdin.read().encode("utf-8")
ts = datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
s3 = boto3.client("s3", endpoint_url="http://s3.airflow.svc.cluster.local:9000", aws_access_key_id="minioadmin", aws_secret_access_key="minioadmin")
s3.put_object(Bucket="airflow-backups", Key="ldap/ldap-backup-latest.ldif", Body=content)
s3.put_object(Bucket="airflow-backups", Key=f"ldap/archive/ldap-backup-{ts}.ldif", Body=content)
print(f"\033[0;32m[SUCCESS] Backed up {len(content)} bytes to s3://airflow-backups/ldap/ldap-backup-latest.ldif and archive/ldap-backup-{ts}.ldif\033[0m")
'
                ;;
            restore)
                echo -e "${CYAN}Restoring OpenLDAP directory from MinIO S3 (s3://airflow-backups/ldap/ldap-backup-latest.ldif)...${NC}"
                kubectl exec -n "${NAMESPACE}" deploy/airflow-scheduler -c scheduler -- python3 -c '
import boto3
s3 = boto3.client("s3", endpoint_url="http://s3.airflow.svc.cluster.local:9000", aws_access_key_id="minioadmin", aws_secret_access_key="minioadmin")
obj = s3.get_object(Bucket="airflow-backups", Key="ldap/ldap-backup-latest.ldif")
print(obj["Body"].read().decode("utf-8"))
' | docker exec -i airflow-openldap ldapadd -c -x -D "cn=admin,dc=example,dc=org" -w admin 2>&1 | grep -v "Already exists" || true
                echo -e "${GREEN}[SUCCESS] LDAP directory restored from S3.${NC}"
                "${ROOT_DIR}/ctl" ldap sync
                ;;
            *)
                echo -e "${RED}Unknown ldap action: ${action}. Use: status, ui, start, add-user, list-users, list-groups, backup, restore, sync${NC}"
                exit 1
                ;;
        esac
        ;;

    bundle)
        shift
        python3 "${ROOT_DIR}/scripts/copy_repo_to_txt.py" "$@"
        ;;

    help|--help|-h)
        usage
        ;;

    *)
        echo -e "${RED}Unknown command: ${cmd}${NC}"
        usage
        exit 1
        ;;
esac

"""Analytics Daily ETL Pipeline DAG.

Owned by the 'analytics' team.
Accessible ONLY by members of AD group 'aid-analytics-auth'.
Executes in 'analytics_pool' with automatic SmtpNotifier failure alerting.
Data-Aware Scheduled via Airflow 3 Asset: s3://airflow-logs/datasets/finance_ledger
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from datetime import datetime

try:
    from airflow.providers.standard.operators.empty import EmptyOperator
except ImportError:
    from airflow.operators.empty import EmptyOperator  # type: ignore

try:
    from airflow.providers.standard.operators.python import PythonOperator
except ImportError:
    from airflow.operators.python import PythonOperator  # type: ignore

try:
    from airflow.sdk import Asset, DAG
except ImportError:
    from airflow.models.dag import DAG  # type: ignore
    try:
        from airflow.datasets import Dataset as Asset  # type: ignore
    except ImportError:
        Asset = None  # type: ignore

log = logging.getLogger(__name__)

FINANCE_LEDGER_ASSET = Asset("s3://airflow-logs/datasets/finance_ledger") if Asset else "s3://airflow-logs/datasets/finance_ledger"


def extract_warehouse_data(**context) -> int:
    """Extract relational data using PostgresHook from enterprise metadata database."""
    log.info("Querying PostgreSQL metadata warehouse via PostgresHook...")
    row_count = 0
    try:
        from airflow.providers.postgres.hooks.postgres import PostgresHook

        pg_hook = PostgresHook(postgres_conn_id="postgres_default")
        records = pg_hook.get_records("SELECT count(*) FROM dag_run;")
        if records and records[0]:
            row_count = int(records[0][0])
            log.info("Successfully queried Postgres. Active historical dag_runs: %d", row_count)
    except Exception as exc:
        log.warning("PostgresHook notice (database offline or connection pending): %s", exc)
        row_count = 42

    return row_count


def transform_metrics(**context) -> dict[str, int]:
    """Perform dimension table lookups and aggregate analytical KPIs."""
    log.info("Transforming staging dimensions into curated data marts...")
    transformed = {
        "active_tenants": 4,
        "processed_partitions": 12,
        "dq_pass_rate_pct": 100,
    }
    log.info("Transform step completed: %s", transformed)
    return transformed


def load_marts(**context) -> None:
    """Load dimensional data into enterprise analytical data marts."""
    log.info("Loading reconciled facts into analytics marts. Refreshing downstream views.")
    log.info("Analytical data mart refresh completed successfully.")


dag_schedule = [FINANCE_LEDGER_ASSET] if Asset else None

with DAG(
    dag_id="analytics_daily_etl",
    start_date=datetime(2026, 1, 1),
    schedule=dag_schedule,
    catchup=False,
    tags=["analytics", "etl"],
    default_args={
        "owner": "analytics",
        "retries": 1,
    },
    doc_md="Data warehouse ETL ingestion jobs triggered by upstream finance ledger asset updates. Access restricted to aid-analytics-auth.",
) as dag:

    start = EmptyOperator(task_id="start")

    extract_task = PythonOperator(
        task_id="extract_warehouse_data",
        python_callable=extract_warehouse_data,
    )

    transform_task = PythonOperator(
        task_id="transform_metrics",
        python_callable=transform_metrics,
    )

    load_task = PythonOperator(
        task_id="load_marts",
        python_callable=load_marts,
    )

    end = EmptyOperator(task_id="end")

    start >> extract_task >> transform_task >> load_task >> end

"""Failure Alerting Drill DAG.

Owned by the 'analytics' team.
Used by './ctl test-alert' to test real Airflow task failure alerting via SmtpNotifier.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from datetime import datetime

try:
    from airflow.providers.standard.operators.empty import EmptyOperator
except ImportError:
    from airflow.operators.empty import EmptyOperator  # type: ignore

try:
    from airflow.providers.standard.operators.python import PythonOperator
except ImportError:
    from airflow.operators.python import PythonOperator  # type: ignore

try:
    from airflow.sdk import DAG
except ImportError:
    from airflow.models.dag import DAG  # type: ignore

log = logging.getLogger(__name__)


def trigger_simulated_failure(**context) -> None:
    """Raise a deliberate error to trigger on_failure_callback (SmtpNotifier)."""
    log.info("Executing simulated failure drill task on Celery worker...")
    raise RuntimeError("Simulated task failure to verify end-to-end Airflow SmtpNotifier alerting pipeline.")


with DAG(
    dag_id="failure_drill",
    start_date=datetime(2026, 1, 1),
    schedule=None,
    catchup=False,
    tags=["analytics", "monitoring", "alerting"],
    default_args={
        "owner": "analytics",
        "retries": 1,
    },
    doc_md="Simulated task failure drill to verify SmtpNotifier email dispatch via Celery worker.",
) as dag:

    start = EmptyOperator(task_id="start")

    fail_task = PythonOperator(
        task_id="test_notification",
        python_callable=trigger_simulated_failure,
    )

    start >> fail_task

"""Finance Monthly Report DAG.

Owned by the 'finance' team.
Accessible ONLY by members of AD group 'aid-finance-auth'.
Executes in 'finance_pool' with automatic SmtpNotifier failure alerting.
Publishes Airflow 3 Asset: s3://airflow-logs/datasets/finance_ledger
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import logging
from datetime import datetime, timezone

try:
    from airflow.providers.standard.operators.empty import EmptyOperator
except ImportError:
    from airflow.operators.empty import EmptyOperator  # type: ignore

try:
    from airflow.providers.standard.operators.python import PythonOperator
except ImportError:
    from airflow.operators.python import PythonOperator  # type: ignore

try:
    from airflow.sdk import Asset, DAG
except ImportError:
    from airflow.models.dag import DAG  # type: ignore
    try:
        from airflow.datasets import Dataset as Asset  # type: ignore
    except ImportError:
        Asset = None  # type: ignore

log = logging.getLogger(__name__)

FINANCE_LEDGER_ASSET = Asset("s3://airflow-logs/datasets/finance_ledger") if Asset else "s3://airflow-logs/datasets/finance_ledger"


def extract_gl_transactions(**context) -> int:
    """Extract general ledger transactions for the financial accounting period."""
    log.info("Extracting general ledger entries from corporate ERP...")
    transactions_count = 1420
    log.info("Successfully extracted %d ledger line items.", transactions_count)
    return transactions_count


def compute_balance_sheet(**context) -> dict[str, float]:
    """Aggregate assets, liabilities, and equity calculations."""
    log.info("Computing departmental asset sheets and reconciliations...")
    balance_summary = {
        "current_assets": 1250000.00,
        "fixed_assets": 850000.00,
        "total_liabilities": 620000.00,
        "net_equity": 1480000.00,
    }
    log.info("Balance sheet calculation complete: %s", balance_summary)
    return balance_summary


def publish_s3_report(**context) -> str:
    """Publish the verified financial balance sheet to MinIO S3 object storage."""
    report_data = {
        "report_id": "FIN-REP-2026-M10",
        "generated_at": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
        "department": "finance",
        "status": "AUDITED_AND_RECONCILED",
        "net_equity": 1480000.00,
    }
    payload = json.dumps(report_data, indent=2)
    bucket = "airflow-logs"
    s3_key = "reports/finance/balance_sheet_latest.json"

    try:
        from airflow.providers.amazon.aws.hooks.s3 import S3Hook

        s3_hook = S3Hook(aws_conn_id="aws_default")
        s3_hook.load_string(
            string_data=payload,
            key=s3_key,
            bucket_name=bucket,
            replace=True,
        )
        log.info("Successfully published financial audit report to s3://%s/%s", bucket, s3_key)
    except Exception as exc:
        log.warning("S3Hook upload notice (minio offline or connection pending): %s", exc)
        log.info("Cached report payload in task execution context: %s", payload)

    return f"s3://{bucket}/{s3_key}"


with DAG(
    dag_id="finance_monthly_report",
    start_date=datetime(2026, 1, 1),
    schedule=None,
    catchup=False,
    tags=["finance", "reporting"],
    default_args={
        "owner": "finance",
        "retries": 1,
    },
    doc_md="Monthly financial balance sheet workflow. Access restricted to aid-finance-auth.",
) as dag:

    start = EmptyOperator(task_id="start")

    extract_task = PythonOperator(
        task_id="extract_gl_transactions",
        python_callable=extract_gl_transactions,
    )

    compute_task = PythonOperator(
        task_id="compute_balance_sheet",
        python_callable=compute_balance_sheet,
    )

    publish_kwargs = {
        "task_id": "publish_s3_report",
        "python_callable": publish_s3_report,
    }
    if Asset:
        publish_kwargs["outlets"] = [FINANCE_LEDGER_ASSET]

    publish_task = PythonOperator(**publish_kwargs)

    end = EmptyOperator(task_id="end")

    start >> extract_task >> compute_task >> publish_task >> end

def pull_ad_spend(**context) -> dict[str, float]:
    """Ingest ad spend metrics across digital acquisition channels."""
    log.info("Pulling ad spend reports from search and social platforms...")
    spend = {
        "search_ads": 15400.00,
        "social_ads": 22100.00,
        "affiliate_network": 4800.00,
    }
    log.info("Total marketing spend retrieved: $%.2f", sum(spend.values()))
    return spend


def calculate_roas(**context) -> dict[str, float]:
    """Calculate return on ad spend (ROAS) against customer conversion events."""
    log.info("Computing multi-touch attribution and ROAS ratios...")
    attribution = {
        "blended_roas": 4.35,
        "search_roas": 5.12,
        "social_roas": 3.82,
    }
    log.info("ROAS evaluation complete: %s", attribution)
    return attribution


def generate_campaign_summary(**context) -> None:
    """Generate executive summary and push conversion stats to internal dashboards."""
    log.info("Publishing marketing performance KPIs for growth committee review.")
    log.info("Campaign metrics dashboard updated.")


with DAG(
    dag_id="marketing_campaign_tracker",

# Slim base so every provider is declared in requirements.txt.
# Python 3.12 is pinned because Python 3.10 reaches end of life in October 2026.
ARG AIRFLOW_VERSION=3.3.2
ARG PYTHON_VERSION=3.12
FROM apache/airflow:slim-${AIRFLOW_VERSION}-python${PYTHON_VERSION}

USER root
RUN apt-get update \
    && apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends curl \
    && apt-get clean \
    && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*

ARG AIRFLOW_VERSION
COPY --chown=airflow:0 constraints/constraints-3.12.txt /opt/airflow/constraints/constraints-3.12.txt
COPY --chown=airflow:0 requirements.txt /opt/airflow/requirements/requirements.txt

USER airflow
RUN pip install --no-cache-dir \
        --constraint /opt/airflow/constraints/constraints-3.12.txt \
        "apache-airflow==${AIRFLOW_VERSION}" \
        -r /opt/airflow/requirements/requirements.txt

COPY --chown=airflow:0 config/ /opt/airflow/config/
COPY --chown=airflow:0 config/webserver_config.py /opt/airflow/webserver_config.py

ENV PYTHONPATH="/opt/airflow/config:${PYTHONPATH}"

USER airflow

# Cluster Access & UI Endpoints Reference

This document provides a complete directory of all **8 Web UIs, Consoles, and Access Endpoints** running in the Apache Airflow 3 Enterprise Kubernetes lab, along with authentication credentials, team personas, and network routing details.

---

## 1. Web UIs & Consoles Directory

All web interfaces are available via **Enterprise TLS Ingress** (HTTPS) and direct **Docker / NodePort** port forwarding (HTTP fallback).

| Service | Secure HTTPS (Ingress) | Direct HTTP (Localhost) | Username / Credentials | Purpose & Capabilities |
|:---|:---|:---|:---|:---|
| **Airflow Web UI** | [https://airflow.local](https://airflow.local)<br>*(or [nip.io](https://airflow.172.18.0.2.nip.io))* | [http://localhost:8080](http://localhost:8080) | Click **"Sign in with keycloak"**<br>*(See Team Personas below)* | Core Airflow UI: DAG orchestration, task execution logs, pipeline triggering, and audit logs. |
| **Grafana Dashboards** | [https://grafana.local](https://grafana.local)<br>*(or [nip.io](https://grafana.172.18.0.2.nip.io))* | `kubectl port-forward svc/grafana 3000:3000 -n observability` | Auto-login (**Admin**)<br>*(or `admin` / `admin`)* | Production observability: scheduler heartbeats, Celery worker capacity, task throughput, and Unified Alerting rules. |
| **Prometheus Query UI** | [https://prometheus.local](https://prometheus.local)<br>*(or [nip.io](https://prometheus.172.18.0.2.nip.io))* | `kubectl port-forward svc/prometheus 9090:9090 -n observability` | *No auth required* | Metrics engine: raw PromQL queries, target scrape health (`/targets`), alert rules, and TSDB time-series analysis. |
| **Keycloak Admin Console** | [https://auth.local](https://auth.local)<br>*(or [nip.io](https://auth.172.18.0.2.nip.io))* | [http://localhost:8081](http://localhost:8081) | **User:** `admin`<br>**Pass:** `admin`<br>*(Realm: `airflow`)* | Corporate Identity Provider: LDAP User Federation, OIDC clients, token lifecycle, and role mappings. |
| **Flower (Celery Monitor)** | [https://flower.local](https://flower.local)<br>*(or [nip.io](https://flower.172.18.0.2.nip.io))* | [http://localhost:5555](http://localhost:5555) | *No auth required* | Celery execution pool monitor: active worker nodes, running tasks, queue latency, and task failure events. |
| **MinIO S3 Console** | [https://minio.local](https://minio.local)<br>*(or [nip.io](https://minio.172.18.0.2.nip.io))* | [http://localhost:9001](http://localhost:9001) | **User:** `minioadmin`<br>**Pass:** `minioadmin` | S3-compatible remote storage: inspect persisted task logs under bucket `s3://airflow-logs`. |
| **HashiCorp Vault UI** | [https://vault.local](https://vault.local)<br>*(or [nip.io](https://vault.172.18.0.2.nip.io))* | [http://localhost:8200](http://localhost:8200) | **Method:** Token<br>**Token:** `airflow-vault-root-token-2026` | Enterprise secrets management: KV v2 secrets engine, connection URIs, AppRole, and Kubernetes auth backends. |
| **phpLDAPadmin** | [https://ldap.local](https://ldap.local)<br>*(or [nip.io](https://ldap.172.18.0.2.nip.io))* | [http://localhost:8082](http://localhost:8082) | **DN:** `cn=admin,dc=example,dc=org`<br>**Pass:** `admin` | Active Directory / LDAP directory tree browser: view/edit users (`ou=users`) and security groups (`ou=groups`). |

---

## 2. Team Personas & Dynamic RBAC Login Matrix

When accessing the Airflow Web UI ([https://airflow.local](https://airflow.local)), click **"Sign in with keycloak"** and sign in using any of the enterprise LDAP personas below:

| Username | Password | Active Directory Group | Visible / Authorized DAGs | Access Level |
|:---|:---|:---|:---|:---|
| **`alice`** | `Alice-lab-1` | `airflow-admins` | **All DAGs** | **Full Administrator** (Can view, trigger, pause, and edit all pipelines). |
| **`user_finance`** | `Finance-lab-1` | `aid-finance-auth` | `finance_monthly_report` | **Finance Team Operator** (Isolated to finance workflows only). |
| **`user_analytics`** | `Analytics-lab-1` | `aid-analytics-auth` | `analytics_daily_etl` | **Analytics Team Operator** (Isolated to analytics data ingestion). |
| **`user_marketing`** | `Marketing-lab-1` | `aid-marketing-auth` | `marketing_campaign_tracker` | **Marketing Team Operator** (Isolated to marketing pipelines). |
| **`user_logistics`** | `Logistics-lab-1` | `aid-logistics-auth` | `logistics_fleet_monitoring` | **Logistics Team Operator** (Isolated to logistics tracking). |
| **`breakglass`** | `ChangeMeInSecret123!` | *Direct DB Admin* | **All DAGs** | **Emergency Break-glass** (Bypasses IdP in case Keycloak/LDAP is offline). |

---

## 3. Network Architecture & DNS Resolution

```mermaid
flowchart TD
    subgraph Browser["Developer Workstation"]
        H1["https://airflow.local"]
        H2["https://auth.local"]
        H3["https://grafana.local"]
        H4["https://*.172.18.0.2.nip.io"]
    end

    subgraph KinD["KinD Cluster Node (172.18.0.2)"]
        NIC["NGINX Ingress Controller (:80 / :443)"]
        CM["cert-manager CA Issuer"]
        CM -.->|Automated TLS Secret| NIC
        
        NIC -->|Route / Host| AirflowWeb["Airflow API / UI (:8080)"]
        NIC -->|Route / Host| Flower["Celery Flower (:5555)"]
        NIC -->|Route / Host| Grafana["Grafana UI (:3000)"]
        NIC -->|Route / Host| Prometheus["Prometheus (:9090)"]
    end

    subgraph ExtBridge["External Docker Bridge (Headless Services)"]
        NIC -->|Route / Host| Keycloak["Keycloak (:8080)"]
        NIC -->|Route / Host| Vault["HashiCorp Vault (:8200)"]
        NIC -->|Route / Host| MinIO["MinIO Console (:9001)"]
        NIC -->|Route / Host| LDAP["phpLDAPadmin (:80)"]
    end

    Browser -->|TLS Handshake| NIC
```

### DNS Mapping Options
1. **Short Clean Domains (`*.local`)**:
   - Configured via `/etc/hosts` pointing to the KinD node IP (`172.18.0.2`).
   - Run `sudo ./ctl hosts` to automatically audit or add the records.
2. **Wildcard Domains (`*.172.18.0.2.nip.io`)**:
   - Zero-configuration fallback that works immediately without modifying system hosts.
   - Example: `https://airflow.172.18.0.2.nip.io`, `https://grafana.172.18.0.2.nip.io`.

### TLS & SSL Certificates
- All HTTPS traffic is terminated at the NGINX Ingress Controller using certificates signed by the cluster root CA (`airflow-ca-issuer`).
- When navigating to `https://*.local` or `https://*.nip.io`, your browser will show a self-signed warning. Click **Advanced → Proceed** (or trust the root CA).

---

## 4. Useful CLI Helpers

The portable `./ctl` CLI provides built-in shortcuts to inspect endpoints and launch services:

```bash
# Print current cluster status and all access URLs
./ctl status

# Verify or add DNS entries in /etc/hosts
sudo ./ctl hosts

# View Ingress routes and TLS certificate statuses
./ctl ingress

# View Prometheus & Grafana dashboard endpoints
./ctl metrics

# View HashiCorp Vault status and root token
./ctl vault ui

# View OpenLDAP / phpLDAPadmin status and login DN
./ctl ldap ui

# Test Keycloak OIDC authentication for all team personas
./ctl test-auth

# Back up OpenLDAP directory to MinIO S3 (s3://airflow-backups/ldap/)
./ctl ldap backup

# Restore OpenLDAP directory from MinIO S3 backup
./ctl ldap restore

# Trigger failure alert email to Gmail (alerts@example.com)
./ctl test-alert

# Trigger Grafana platform health alert email to Gmail
./ctl test-grafana-alert
```

kind: Secret
metadata:
  name: airflow-keda-db-secret

stringData:
  connection: "postgresql://airflow:airflow@postgres.airflow.svc.cluster.local:5432/airflow?sslmode=disable"

apiVersion: keda.sh/v1alpha1
kind: TriggerAuthentication
metadata:
  name: keda-trigger-auth-db
  namespace: airflow
spec:
  secretTargetRef:
    - parameter: connection
      name: airflow-keda-db-secret
      key: connection
---
apiVersion: keda.sh/v1alpha1
kind: ScaledObject
metadata:
  name: airflow-worker
  namespace: airflow
  labels:
    tier: airflow
    component: worker-horizontalpodautoscaler
    deploymentName: airflow-worker
spec:
  scaleTargetRef:
    kind: StatefulSet
    name: airflow-worker
  pollingInterval: 5
  cooldownPeriod: 30
  minReplicaCount: 1
  maxReplicaCount: 5
  triggers:
    - type: postgresql
      metadata:
        targetQueryValue: "1"
        query: "SELECT ceil(COUNT(*)::decimal / 16) FROM task_instance WHERE (state='running' OR state='queued') AND queue IN ('default')"
      authenticationRef:
        name: keda-trigger-auth-db

  kubeadmConfigPatches:
  - |
    kind: InitConfiguration
    nodeRegistration:
      kubeletExtraArgs:
        node-labels: "ingress-ready=true"
  extraPortMappings:
  # Ingress HTTP
  - containerPort: 80
    hostPort: 80
    protocol: TCP
  # Ingress HTTPS (TLS)
  - containerPort: 443
    hostPort: 443
    protocol: TCP
  # Map host port 8080 to NodePort 30080 for Airflow Web UI (legacy/fallback)
  - containerPort: 30080
    hostPort: 8080
    protocol: TCP
  # Map host port 5555 to NodePort 30555 for Flower UI (legacy/fallback)
  - containerPort: 30555
    hostPort: 5555

apiVersion: v1
kind: Namespace
metadata:
  name: observability
  labels:
    kubernetes.io/metadata.name: observability
---
apiVersion: networking.k8s.io/v1
kind: NetworkPolicy
metadata:
  name: allow-prometheus-scrape-statsd
  namespace: airflow
spec:
  podSelector:
    matchLabels:
      tier: airflow
      component: statsd
  policyTypes:
    - Ingress
  ingress:
    - from:
        - namespaceSelector:
            matchLabels:
              kubernetes.io/metadata.name: observability
      ports:
        - protocol: TCP
          port: 9102
---
apiVersion: v1
kind: ConfigMap
metadata:
  name: prometheus-config
  namespace: observability
data:
  prometheus.yml: |
    global:
      scrape_interval: 5s
      evaluation_interval: 5s

    scrape_configs:
      - job_name: "airflow-statsd"
        metrics_path: /metrics
        static_configs:
          - targets: ["airflow-statsd.airflow.svc.cluster.local:9102"]
            labels:
              app: airflow
              env: production-lab

      - job_name: "prometheus"
        static_configs:
          - targets: ["localhost:9090"]
---
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: prometheus
  namespace: observability
  labels:
    app: prometheus
spec:
  replicas: 1
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app: prometheus
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        app: prometheus
    spec:
      containers:
        - name: prometheus
          image: prom/prometheus:v2.54.1
          args:
            - "--config.file=/etc/prometheus/prometheus.yml"
            - "--storage.tsdb.path=/prometheus"
            - "--web.enable-lifecycle"
          ports:
            - name: web
              containerPort: 9090
          resources:
            limits:
              cpu: "500m"
              memory: "512Mi"
            requests:
              cpu: "100m"
              memory: "128Mi"
          volumeMounts:
            - name: config
              mountPath: /etc/prometheus
            - name: storage
              mountPath: /prometheus
      volumes:
        - name: config
          configMap:
            name: prometheus-config
        - name: storage
          emptyDir: {}
---

# Project Context & Session Handover: Airflow 3 + Keycloak Local Kubernetes Lab

> **Purpose**: This document provides complete context, architectural blueprints, configuration state, resolved issues, credentials, and development rules for resuming work seamlessly in a new conversation session.

---

## 1. Executive Summary & Objective

This repository contains a production-grade, local Kubernetes (**KinD**) implementation of **Apache Airflow 3.3.2** integrated with **Keycloak IAM (v26.0.7)** for Enterprise Single Sign-On (SSO) and **Owner-Based Dynamic Role-Based Access Control (RBAC)**.

### Primary Objectives Achieved:
1. **Dynamic Owner-Based RBAC**: Airflow DAGs automatically map their `owner` keyword (e.g., `default_args={"owner": "finance"}`) to Active Directory (AD) security groups following the pattern `aid-<keyword>-auth` (e.g., `aid-finance-auth`).
2. **Strict DAG Multi-Tenant Isolation**: Each department user has full control (read, edit, trigger, delete, view task logs) over their owned DAG, and **zero access/visibility** to any other department's DAGs.
3. **Modern Airflow 3 React UI Compatibility**: Fully resolved all Fast-API / Uvicorn frontend 403 Forbidden errors by establishing seeded operational base permissions without breaking DAG isolation.
4. **Keycloak OIDC Single Sign-On**: Resolved hybrid localhost browser redirects and Kubernetes internal backchannel token verification (`iss` issuer matching).
5. **Component Secret Synchronization**: Unified Fernet and Webserver/API secret keys across scheduler, api-server, and Celery workers to enable log streaming without token mismatches.

---

## 2. System Architecture & Topology

All core services run within the local KinD cluster (`airflow-local`) under the `airflow` namespace:

```
                                  [ User Browser ]
                                         │
             ┌───────────────────────────┴───────────────────────────┐
             │ http://localhost:8080                                 │ http://localhost:8081
             ▼                                                       ▼
  ┌─────────────────────────────────────┐               ┌─────────────────────────────────────┐
  │         Airflow Web / API           │               │            Keycloak IAM             │
  │     (FastAPI / Uvicorn / UI)        │               │       (Realm: 'airflow')            │
  │  NodePort 30080 -> Port 8080        │               │    NodePort 30081 -> Port 8080      │
  └──────────────────┬──────────────────┘               └──────────────────┬──────────────────┘
                     │                                                     │
                     │ OIDC Token & UserInfo Verification (Backchannel)    │
                     │ http://keycloak.airflow.svc.cluster.local:8080       │
                     └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
                                         │
      ┌──────────────────────────────────┼──────────────────────────────────┐
      ▼                                  ▼                                  ▼
┌──────────────────┐           ┌──────────────────┐               ┌──────────────────┐
│ Airflow          │           │ Celery Worker    │               │ DAG Processor    │
│ Scheduler        │           │ (Workload Pods)  │               │ (Policy Engine)  │
└────────┬─────────┘           └────────┬─────────┘               └────────┬─────────┘
         │                              │                                  │
         └──────────────────────────────┼──────────────────────────────────┘
                                        ▼
                      ┌────────────────────────────────────┐
                      │ PostgreSQL 16 (Metadata Database)  │
                      │ Redis 7.2     (Celery Broker)      │
                      └────────────────────────────────────┘
```

### Port Mapping & Access Points:
| Service | Role | Local URL | In-Cluster Service |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Airflow Web UI** | React Frontend & API Server | [http://localhost:8080](http://localhost:8080) | `airflow-webserver:8080` / `airflow-api-server:8080` |
| **Keycloak IAM** | Identity Provider / SSO | [http://localhost:8081](http://localhost:8081) | `keycloak.airflow.svc.cluster.local:8080` |
| **phpLDAPadmin** | Enterprise LDAP Web GUI | [http://localhost:8082](http://localhost:8082) | N/A (Admin: `cn=admin,dc=example,dc=org` / `admin`) |
| **OpenLDAP Server** | Corporate Active Directory Simulator | `ldap://localhost:389` | `openldap.airflow.svc.cluster.local:389` |
| **HashiCorp Vault** | External Secrets Manager | [http://localhost:8200](http://localhost:8200) | `vault.airflow.svc.cluster.local:8200` |
| **MinIO Console** | S3 Remote Log Storage UI | [http://localhost:9001](http://localhost:9001) | N/A (User: `minioadmin` / `minioadmin`) |
| **MinIO S3 API** | S3 API Endpoint | [http://localhost:9000](http://localhost:9000) | `s3.airflow.svc.cluster.local:9000` |
| **PostgreSQL 16** | Metadata DB | Cluster Internal | `postgres.airflow.svc.cluster.local:5432` |
| **Redis 7.2** | Celery Broker | Cluster Internal | `redis.airflow.svc.cluster.local:6379` |
| **Gmail Alerts** | Outbound Failure SMTP | Outbound | `smtp.gmail.com:587` (STARTTLS) |

## 3. Credentials & RBAC Access Matrix

### Enterprise Users (Sourced from OpenLDAP, Federated via Keycloak SSO):
Users are **stored exclusively in OpenLDAP** (`dc=example,dc=org`) and federated automatically into Keycloak. Keycloak has **no manual user entries** other than the breakglass admin.
To log in, visit [http://localhost:8080](http://localhost:8080) and click **"Sign in with keycloak"**.

| Username | Password | AD Group (`aid-<keyword>-auth`) | Owned DAG ID | Access Level |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `alice` | `Alice-lab-1` | `airflow-admins` | **All DAGs** | Super Admin (full cluster control) |
| `user_finance` | `Finance-lab-1` | `aid-finance-auth` | `finance_monthly_report` | Owner (read, edit, trigger, delete) |
| `user_marketing` | `Marketing-lab-1` | `aid-marketing-auth` | `marketing_campaign_tracker` | Owner (read, edit, trigger, delete) |
| `user_logistics` | `Logistics-lab-1` | `aid-logistics-auth` | `logistics_fleet_monitoring` | Owner (read, edit, trigger, delete) |
| `user_analytics` | `Analytics-lab-1` | `aid-analytics-auth` | `analytics_daily_etl` | Owner (read, edit, trigger, delete) |

### Administrative Backdoors & Directory Management:
- **phpLDAPadmin Web GUI**: [http://localhost:8082](http://localhost:8082) (Login DN: `cn=admin,dc=example,dc=org` | Pass: `admin`). Used to visually create new users, add them to AD groups, and delete employees.
- **Break-glass Local DB Admin**: `breakglass` / `ChangeMeInSecret123!` (Direct Airflow database account, bypasses Keycloak/LDAP).
- **Keycloak Admin Console**: `admin` / `admin` at [http://localhost:8081](http://localhost:8081).

---

## 4. Key Architectural Patterns & Implementations

### A. Dynamic Owner-Based RBAC (`config/airflow_local_settings.py`)
Rather than manually hardcoding `access_control` inside every DAG file, an Airflow Cluster Policy intercepts every DAG during parsing:
1. Extracts `owner = dag.default_args.get("owner")` or `dag.owner`.
2. Normalizes keyword to lowercase (e.g. `finance`).
3. Computes the target role: `f"aid-{keyword}-auth"`.
4. Injects permissions dynamically into `dag.access_control[target_role] = {"can_read", "can_edit", "can_delete"}`.
5. Also inspects DAG `tags` for any tag matching `aid-*-auth` or specific team keywords.

### B. Seeded Base Permissions for Airflow 3 React UI (`scripts/start-local-k8s.sh`)
In Airflow 3, the React frontend (`/ui/dags`) performs automated background queries for execution state. If a custom role lacks base permissions, the UI displays a red **403 Forbidden** banner.
During cluster startup, `start-local-k8s.sh` automatically seeds the following permissions to each `aid-*-auth` role without granting global DAG read rights:
- Resources: `Asset Aliases`, `Assets`, `Backfills`, `Cluster Activity`, `DAG Code`, `DAG Dependencies`, `DAG Runs`, `DAG Versions`, `DAG Warnings`, `HITL Detail`, `ImportError`, `Jobs`, `My Password`, `My Profile`, `Pools`, `Plugins`, `SLA Misses`, `Task Instances`, `Task Logs`, `Website`, `XComs`.
- Actions: `can_read`, `can_create`, `can_edit`, `can_delete` (scoped to their owned task instances/runs), and required `menu_access`.

### C. Keycloak Hostname & Backchannel Issuer Resolution (`k8s/keycloak/deployment.yaml`)
To solve OIDC `iss` issuer mismatches between host-browser port forwards (`http://localhost:8081`) and in-cluster communication (`http://keycloak.airflow.svc.cluster.local:8080`), Keycloak was configured with:
```yaml
- name: KC_HOSTNAME
  value: "http://localhost:8081"
- name: KC_HOSTNAME_BACKCHANNEL_DYNAMIC
  value: "true"
```
This forces Keycloak to issue all JWT tokens with `iss: http://localhost:8081/realms/airflow`, matching the Authlib client discovery configuration.

### D. Component Secret Key Synchronization (`values.yaml`)
Airflow 3 signs and verifies worker task log tokens using `webserverSecretKey` / `apiSecretKey`. To prevent signature verification failures when reading worker logs from the UI/API server, static keys are pinned:
```yaml
webserverSecretKey: "2SL0kSlBxSYcpJnwzhBCWFlGf0mxEao2"
apiSecretKey: "2SL0kSlBxSYcpJnwzhBCWFlGf0mxEao2"
```

---

## 5. Repository Authoring Rules & Guidelines

When authoring new DAGs or modifying code in this repository, you **MUST** follow these non-negotiable rules:

1. **NO TaskFlow API**:
   - **NEVER** use `@dag`, `@task`, `@task.python`, `@task.bash`, or any other TaskFlow decorators.
   - **ALWAYS** define DAGs using the standard context manager:
     ```python
     with DAG(
         dag_id="example_dag",
         default_args={"owner": "finance"},
         ...
     ) as dag:
     ```
   - **ALWAYS** instantiate explicit Operator classes: `PythonOperator`, `BashOperator`, `EmptyOperator`, `EmailOperator`, etc.
2. **Explicit Bitshift Chaining**:
   - Task dependencies must always be chained using bitshift operators: `task1 >> task2 >> task3`.
3. **No Hardcoded `access_control` in DAGs**:
   - Do not write `access_control={...}` inside DAG definitions. The cluster policy in `config/airflow_local_settings.py` applies permissions automatically based on `owner`.
4. **Shell Command Audit Logging**:
   - Every shell command executed must be prepended to the `## Entries` section in [`agent-command.md`](file:///workspace/Learn_Airflow/agent-command.md).

## 7. Operational Runbook

### Starting the Lab:
```bash
./ctl start
```
*What this does automatically:*
1. Checks for Docker and prerequisites.
2. Builds `custom-airflow:3.3.2-lab` Docker image.
3. Provisions KinD cluster `airflow-local` with port forwards (`8080`, `8081`).
4. Loads custom image into KinD.
5. Deploys Keycloak, mounts `realm-airflow.json`, and waits for readiness.
6. Deploys Airflow 3.3.2 using Helm with `values.yaml`.
7. Automatically seeds AD team roles (`aid-*-auth`) and base execution permissions.

### Checking Cluster Status:
```bash
./ctl status
```

### Validating OIDC SSO Backchannel Token:
```bash
./ctl test-auth
```
*Verifies Keycloak returns a valid JWT access token and userinfo payload for `user_finance`.*

### Testing Outbound Email Failure Alerts:
```bash
./ctl test-alert
```
*Triggers `failure_drill` task failure to verify end-to-end Airflow SmtpNotifier dispatch to `alerts@example.com`.*

### Viewing Component Logs:
```bash
./ctl logs worker       # Follow worker logs
./ctl logs scheduler    # Follow scheduler logs
./ctl logs keycloak     # Follow Keycloak logs
```

### Stopping / Tearing Down the Cluster:
```bash
./ctl stop
```

- **Cluster State**: Fully operational KinD cluster (`airflow-local`) with 10 pods Running.
- **Git-Sync**: Enabled via `values.yaml` targeting `git@github.com:your-org/airflow-enterprise-lab.git` on branch `main` (subpath: `dags`) with a 30-second sync interval (`period: 30s` / `wait: 30`). Authentication managed via Kubernetes secret `airflow-ssh-secret`.
- **Keycloak & Auth**: All 6 accounts (`alice`, `breakglass`, `user_finance`, `user_marketing`, `user_logistics`, `user_analytics`) provisioned and verified in Keycloak.
- **Immediate Next Steps**:
  1. Continue developing or testing DAG pipelines.
  2. Any changes pushed to GitHub `origin/main` under `dags/` will now automatically sync to Airflow within 30 seconds.

# Apache Airflow 3 — Enterprise Kubernetes Lab

> Production-grade **Apache Airflow 3.3.2** running on local Kubernetes (**KinD**),
> wired to a full enterprise security & infrastructure stack:
> **OpenLDAP → Keycloak OIDC SSO → Dynamic Owner-Based RBAC**,
> **HashiCorp Vault** secrets management, **MinIO S3** remote logging,
> external **PostgreSQL 16** + **Redis 7**, and **Gmail SMTP** failure alerts.

Everything runs locally with a single command — no cloud accounts required.

---

## Architecture Overview

The lab mirrors an enterprise deployment pattern where **stateful infrastructure
services live outside the compute cluster** and are bridged in via headless
Kubernetes Services + Endpoints. Airflow's compute workloads (scheduler, workers,
API server, DAG processor, triggerer), platform controllers (NGINX Ingress,
cert-manager CA, KEDA autoscaler), and observability (Prometheus + Grafana) run
inside a KinD cluster, while databases, brokers, identity providers, and secret
stores run as standalone Docker containers.

### High-Level Topology

```mermaid
graph TB
    subgraph CLIENT["Clients & Workstations"]
        BROWSER["Developer Browser / CLI<br/><i>https://airflow.local | https://auth.local</i>"]
    end

    subgraph KIND["KinD Cluster — airflow-local"]
        direction TB

        subgraph INGRESS_LAYER["Ingress & Security Layer (TLS)"]
            ING["NGINX Ingress Controller<br/><i>Terminates TLS (Port 80/443)</i>"]
            CERT["cert-manager<br/><i>Internal Root CA Issuer</i>"]
        end

        subgraph AIRFLOW_LAYER["Airflow Compute Layer (namespace/airflow)"]
            API["API Server<br/><i>x2 replicas (FastAPI/Uvicorn)</i>"]
            SCH["Scheduler<br/><i>x2 replicas (HA)</i>"]
            DAG["DAG Processor<br/><i>x1 replica (Airflow 3 standalone)</i>"]
            WRK["Celery Workers<br/><i>Dynamic 1-5 replicas (KEDA HPA)</i>"]
            TRG["Triggerer<br/><i>x1 replica (async deferral)</i>"]
            FLW["Flower<br/><i>Celery Monitor</i>"]
            STATSD["StatsD Exporter<br/><i>Prometheus Metrics Bridge</i>"]

        end

        subgraph AUTOSCALE_LAYER["Autoscaling Layer (namespace/keda)"]
            KEDA["KEDA Operator + HPA<br/><i>Watches PostgreSQL Task Queue</i>"]
        end

        subgraph OBS_LAYER["Observability Layer (namespace/observability)"]
            PROM["Prometheus<br/><i>Scrapes StatsD every 5s</i>"]
            GRAF["Grafana 11<br/><i>Airflow Health Dashboards</i>"]
        end
    end

    subgraph EXT["External Infrastructure (Docker Containers)"]
        direction LR
        PG["PostgreSQL 16<br/><i>Metadata DB</i>"]
        RD["Redis 7<br/><i>Celery Broker</i>"]
        KC["Keycloak 26<br/><i>OIDC IdP</i>"]
        LDAP["OpenLDAP<br/><i>Enterprise Directory</i>"]
        VLT["HashiCorp Vault<br/><i>Secrets Engine</i>"]
        MINIO["MinIO<br/><i>S3-compatible Storage</i>"]
        PHA["phpLDAPadmin<br/><i>LDAP Web GUI</i>"]
    end

    GMAIL["Gmail SMTP<br/><i>smtp.gmail.com:587</i>"]
    GH["GitHub<br/><i>DAG Source Repo</i>"]

    BROWSER --> ING
    ING -->|TLS SNI airflow.local| API
    ING -->|TLS SNI auth.local| KC
    ING -->|TLS SNI flower.local| FLW
    ING -->|TLS SNI grafana.local| GRAF

    CERT -.->|Auto-provisions TLS Secrets| ING

    API --- PG
    SCH --- PG
    SCH --- RD
    WRK --- PG
    WRK --- RD
    KEDA -->|Monitors task_instance queue| PG
    KEDA -->|Scales replicas| WRK

    API --- KC
    KC --- LDAP
    SCH --- VLT
    WRK --- VLT
    WRK --- MINIO
    SCH --- MINIO
    WRK --> GMAIL
    GIT --> GH
```
